# L20 Site-wide Em-dash and AI-tell Sweep

Date: 2026-08-02
Branch: `agent/l20-sitewide-ai-tell-sweep-20260802`
Baseline commit: `13d20e401b7c000847b1dddc559de2a2a5ea83dc` (origin/static-migration)
Scope: all 433 indexable rendered routes plus repository content sources; `src/data/wp-export/` remained byte-identical.

## Executive result

| Gate | Result | Evidence |
|---|---:|---|
| Indexable routes | PASS | 433/433 returned 200; baseline 433 |
| Rendered em dashes outside Rules F/G | PASS | 0; 7 Rule G placeholders retained |
| Mojibake | PASS | 832 before; 0 after |
| Phase 3a catalogue | PASS | 25 applicable swaps; final safe-tell count 0 |
| L3 numbers and prices | PASS | 0 token mismatches |
| L3 primary-keyword strings | PASS | 0 title/H1 lexical mismatches |
| Word-count guard | PASS WITH DISCLOSED SAFE-SWAP EDGE | 432/433 at or below 1%; see detail |
| WordPress export bytes | PASS | 581 files; SHA-256 `19f083a0a9497690ae57a89503a404b4e6f7a88598a756135ab9586108e928b2` |
| TypeScript / lint / build / postbuild | PASS | `npm run build` completed; 450 total, 433 indexable, 17 intentional redirects |
| Layout / component structure / schema types | PASS | copy strings and shared normalization only; no layout or schema-type additions/removals |

The one raw word-count edge is `/best-porta-cabins-in-bangalore`: 1,758 to 1,740 words (18 words, 1.024%). The complete change is explained by mandatory Phase 3a reductions on that route, including deletion of the sentence containing “look no further”; no additional sentence or section disappeared. All other routes are within 1%.
